The application binaries do **not** run database migrations automatically: `deploy.sh` must idempotently apply the `clearledger` PostgreSQL schema (`clearledger.settlements`, `clearledger.events`, `clearledger.outbox`, `clearledger.idempotency_keys`), all required relational `CHECK` / `UNIQUE` / `FOREIGN KEY` constraints, all state-transition, cross-table, and append-only triggers across all four tables, and its three indexes as defined in `services/rds.md` before `GET /health/ready` reports `200 OK` (`checks.postgres = \"UP\"`). During `POST /v1/settlements` and `POST /v1/settlements/{id}/entries`, `clearledger-api` maps PostgreSQL constraint and trigger exceptions (`SQLSTATE 23514`, `23503`, `23502`, `23505`, and `P0001`) to HTTP `400 Bad Request` (`invalid_settlement`).\n\n## 2. Projector Worker (`projector_image` on AWS Lambda)\n\n- **Bootstrap**: `/var/runtime/bootstrap`\n- **Trigger**: SQS event source mapping from the main SQS queue (`batch_size = 5`, `maximum_batching_window_in_seconds = 0`, `function_response_types = [\"ReportBatchItemFailures\"]`)\n- **Required environment variables**:\n  - `AWS_REGION`: region from `config.json`\n  - `AWS_DEFAULT_REGION`: region from `config.json`\n  - `AWS_ACCESS_KEY_ID`: `\"test\"`\n  - `AWS_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY`: `\"test\"`\n  - `AWS_ENDPOINT_URL`: `\"http://aws:4566\"`\n  - `PROJECTION_TABLE`: name of the DynamoDB projection table\n  - `VALKEY_URL`: `redis://<valkey_host>:<valkey_port>`\n  - `CLOUDWATCH_LOG_GROUP`: name of the projector CloudWatch Log Group\n\nFor each valid event envelope in the SQS batch:\n- Inserts the event item (`PK = \"SETTLEMENT#<settlement_id>\"`, `SK = \"EVENT#<8-digit-zero-padded-version>\"`) in DynamoDB using a conditional `attribute_not_exists(PK) AND attribute_not_exists(SK)` write.\n- Updates the aggregate state item (`PK = \"SETTLEMENT#<settlement_id>\"`, `SK = \"STATE\"`, `GSI1PK = \"ACCOUNT#<account_id>\"`, `GSI1SK = \"SETTLEMENT#<settlement_id>\"`) using a conditional write only when `aggregateVersion` is strictly greater than the stored `version`. Duplicate or older versions are ignored idempotently; if an older version (such as `v1`) arrives after a newer version, any missing static metadata (`reference`, `debit_party`, `credit_party`) is backfilled without regressing `version`, `status`, or `clearing_stage`.\n- Deletes the Valkey key `clearledger:settlement:<settlement_id>`.\n- If any record fails validation or deserialization, its `messageId` is returned in `batchItemFailures` so SQS retries only the failed message and routes poison messages to the DLQ after `maxReceiveCount = 4` receives.\n\n## 3. Outbox Relay Worker (`relay_image` on AWS Lambda)\n\n- **Bootstrap**: `/var/runtime/bootstrap`\n- **Trigger**: EventBridge Scheduler every 1 minute (`rate(1 minute)`)\n- **Required environment variables**:\n  - `AWS_REGION`: region from `config.json`\n  - `AWS_DEFAULT_REGION`: region from `config.json`\n  - `AWS_ACCESS_KEY_ID`: `\"test\"`\n  - `AWS_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY`: `\"test\"`\n  - `AWS_ENDPOINT_URL`: `\"http://aws:4566\"`\n  - `DATABASE_URL`: `postgres://<db_username>:<db_password>@<rds_host>:<rds_port>/<db_name>`\n  - `SQS_QUEUE_URL`: URL of the main SQS event queue\n  - `OUTBOX_BATCH_SIZE`: `\"50\"` (must be explicitly set to `\"50\"`)\n  - `CLOUDWATCH_LOG_GROUP`: name of the relay CloudWatch Log Group\n\nQueries up to `OUTBOX_BATCH_SIZE` rows from `clearledger.outbox` where `published_at IS NULL` ordered by `seq ASC`, sends each event envelope to `SQS_QUEUE_URL`, and updates `published_at = NOW(), attempts = attempts + 1, last_error = NULL`.\n\n## 4. Audit Archiver Worker (`archiver_image` on AWS Lambda)\n\n- **Bootstrap**: `/var/runtime/bootstrap`\n- **Trigger**: EventBridge Scheduler every 5 minutes (`rate(5 minutes)`)\n- **Required environment variables**:\n  - `AWS_REGION`: region from `config.json`\n  - `AWS_DEFAULT_REGION`: region from `config.json`\n  - `AWS_ACCESS_KEY_ID`: `\"test\"`\n  - `AWS_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY`: `\"test\"`\n  - `AWS_ENDPOINT_URL`: `\"http://aws:4566\"`\n  - `DATABASE_URL`: `postgres://<db_username>:<db_password>@<rds_host>:<rds_port>/<db_name>`\n  - `AUDIT_BUCKET`: name of the S3 audit archive bucket\n  - `AUDIT_PREFIX`: `\"ledger-audit/\"` (must be explicitly set to `\"ledger-audit/\"`)\n  - `AUDIT_BATCH_SIZE` (optional): defaults to `100`\n  - `CLOUDWATCH_LOG_GROUP`: name of the archiver CloudWatch Log Group\n\nSelects published outbox rows (`published_at IS NOT NULL AND archived_at IS NULL`) ordered by `seq ASC`, writes a newline-delimited JSON object to `s3://<AUDIT_BUCKET>/ledger-audit/batch-<first_seq:08d>-<last_seq:08d>-<16_char_sha256_hex>.ndjson`, and sets `archived_at = NOW()`.\n\n## 5. OAuth2 Scopes and Authorization\n\nAll protected endpoints validate Bearer JWTs issued by the Cognito User Pool against `AUTH_ISSUER`, `AUTH_JWKS_URL`, and `AUTH_AUDIENCES`. Scopes are strictly non-hierarchical:\n\n- `clearledger/read`: permitted only on `GET /v1/settlements/{id}` and `GET /v1/settlements/{id}/ledger`.\n- `clearledger/write`: permitted only on `POST /v1/settlements` and `POST /v1/settlements/{id}/entries`.\n- `clearledger/admin`: permitted only on `POST /v1/admin/projections/{id}/rebuild`.\n\nMissing or invalid tokens return `401 Unauthorized`; valid tokens lacking the exact endpoint scope return `403 Forbidden`.\n\n## 6. Recovery Drills, Bidirectional Data-Plane Reconciliation, and Lifecycle\n\n- **Backlog & DLQ**: When the projector event source mapping is disabled, API writes commit to PostgreSQL and enqueue on SQS while `GET /v1/settlements/{id}` returns `404` until re-enabled. Duplicate, out-of-order, and concurrent event deliveries are handled without version regression; invalid envelopes are retried and routed to the DLQ after `maxReceiveCount = 4`.\n- **Bidirectional Multi-Store Convergence in `deploy.sh`**:\n  - PostgreSQL (`clearledger.settlements`, `clearledger.events`, `clearledger.outbox`) is the authoritative system of record; DynamoDB, Valkey, and S3 are derived read/archive stores.\n  - Because `clearledger-projector` writes `EVENT#*` items conditionally with `attribute_not_exists(PK) AND attribute_not_exists(SK)` and updates `STATE` conditionally only when `aggregateVersion > current_version`, and because `clearledger-audit-archiver` only archives rows where `published_at IS NOT NULL AND archived_at IS NULL`, `deploy.sh` must converge both the control plane and all derived data stores against PostgreSQL before exiting `0`:\n    - All unpublished outbox rows (`published_at IS NULL`) must be relayed to SQS.\n    - The DynamoDB projection table must contain **only** partitions for settlements that exist in `clearledger.settlements`, and each partition `PK = \"SETTLEMENT#<settlement_id>\"` must contain **only** `SK = \"STATE\"` and `SK = \"EVENT#00000001\" .. \"EVENT#<version:08d>\"` with every attribute (including `AccountIndex` `GSI1PK` / `GSI1SK`, `updated_at`, `last_entry_id`, `last_memo`, and each event's `entry_id`, `memo`, `occurred_at`, `correlation_id`, and `envelope`) matching PostgreSQL.\n    - Valkey (`clearledger:settlement:<settlement_id>`) must not retain orphan keys, entries without a valid TTL in `(0, 90]`, or cached `SettlementProjection` JSON payloads that diverge from the authoritative projection state.\n    - The S3 audit archive bucket (`s3://<AUDIT_BUCKET>`) must contain objects **only** under `ledger-audit/`, every object key and body must conform to `ledger-audit/batch-<first_seq:08d>-<last_seq:08d>-<16_char_sha256_hex>.ndjson` with records ordered by strictly ascending `clearledger.outbox.seq`, the union of all S3 batch records must match `clearledger.outbox` **1-to-1** (every committed outbox event present in S3 **exactly once** with its exact canonical JSON `payload`, and `archived_at IS NOT NULL` in PostgreSQL), and all noncurrent object `Versions` (`IsLatest = false`) and `DeleteMarkers` (`ListObjectVersions` / `DeleteObject` with `VersionId`) must be permanently purged from the versioned bucket so only the single current version (`IsLatest = true`) of each canonical batch remains.\n    - Any out-of-band inline policies (`DeleteRolePolicy`) or attached customer-managed/AWS-managed policies (`DetachRolePolicy`) added to any of the six ClearLedger IAM roles outside Terraform/OpenTofu, as well as any unrestricted `0.0.0.0/0` or `::/0` egress rules on the `alb`, `rds`, or `valkey` security groups, must be removed during `deploy.sh` convergence.\n- **Prefix-Scoped Teardown in `destroy.sh`**:\n  - `destroy.sh` must cleanly tear down all Terraform-managed resources as well as any out-of-band operational resources or policy attachments/versions scoped to `<resource_prefix>` (by name prefix or `ClearLedgerDeployment = <resource_prefix>` tag, including customer-managed KMS keys scheduled for deletion) while keeping all `cl-base-*` baseline resources intact.\n- **Structured Logs**: All four workloads emit JSON log lines containing `correlationId` (propagated from `X-Correlation-Id`) to their configured CloudWatch Log Groups without leaking `db_password` or Cognito `client_secret` values.\n\n=== infrastructure.md\n# ClearLedger Infrastructure Contract\n\nProvision the ClearLedger clearing and settlement platform using Terraform or OpenTofu in `/workspace/submission/infra` with local state stored at `/workspace/submission/infra/terraform.tfstate`.\n\n## Required Input Source\n\nRead runtime inputs from `/workspace/config/config.json`:\n\n- `resource_prefix`: unique prefix for this deployment (for example `cl-a1b2c3`)\n- `region`: AWS region (`us-east-1`)\n- `aws_endpoint_url`: AWS control-plane endpoint (`http://aws:4566`)\n- `db_name`, `db_username`, `db_password`: PostgreSQL database name and credentials\n- `api_image`, `projector_image`, `relay_image`, `archiver_image`: container image references\n- `api_image_id`, `projector_image_id`, `relay_image_id`, `archiver_image_id`: image digest identifiers\n\nEvery taggable AWS resource created by your configuration must carry the tag `ClearLedgerDeployment = <resource_prefix>` (at least 25 tagged resources) and have its name or identifier prefixed with `<resource_prefix>`. All cloud resources and IAM policies must be declared in Terraform/OpenTofu (`aws_vpc`, `aws_subnet`, `aws_internet_gateway`, `aws_route_table`, `aws_security_group`, `aws_lb`, `aws_lb_target_group`, `aws_lb_listener`, `aws_ecs_cluster`, `aws_ecs_task_definition`, `aws_ecs_service`, `aws_db_subnet_group`, `aws_db_instance`, `aws_dynamodb_table`, `aws_elasticache_subnet_group`, `aws_elasticache_replication_group`, `aws_s3_bucket`, `aws_s3_bucket_versioning`, `aws_s3_bucket_server_side_encryption_configuration`, `aws_s3_bucket_public_access_block`, `aws_sqs_queue`, `aws_lambda_function`, `aws_lambda_event_source_mapping`, `aws_scheduler_schedule`, `aws_cognito_user_pool`, `aws_cognito_resource_server`, `aws_cognito_user_pool_client`, `aws_iam_role`, `aws_iam_role_policy`, `aws_kms_key`, `aws_kms_alias`, `aws_cloudwatch_log_group`) rather than created via imperative `aws` CLI commands in `deploy.sh`.\n\n## Service Specifications\n\nRead the individual service contracts in `/workspace/contracts/services/` for the exact resource attributes and wiring requirements:\n\n- [VPC and Security Groups](services/vpc.md)\n- [Application Load Balancer](services/alb.md)\n- [ECS Fargate Service](services/ecs.md)\n- [RDS PostgreSQL](services/rds.md)\n- [SQS Main Queue and DLQ](services/sqs.md)\n- [Lambda Workers and Event Source Mapping](services/lambda.md)\n- [DynamoDB Projection Store](services/dynamodb.md)\n- [ElastiCache for Valkey](services/elasticache-valkey.md)\n- [S3 Audit Archive](services/s3.md)\n- [EventBridge Scheduler](services/scheduler.md)\n- [Cognito User Pool and OAuth2 Scopes](services/cognito.md)\n- [IAM Roles and Least-Privilege Policies](services/iam.md)\n- [Customer-Managed KMS Keys](services/kms.md)\n- [CloudWatch Log Groups](services/cloudwatch-logs.md)\n\n## Submission Scripts and Manifest\n\n1. `/workspace/submission/deploy.sh`:\n   - Must be executable and idempotent (timeout: 720s).\n   - Runs `terraform` or `tofu` against `/workspace/submission/infra` with state saved at `/workspace/submission/infra/terraform.tfstate`.\n   - Idempotently initializes the `clearledger` PostgreSQL schema, tables, relational constraints, triggers, and indexes on the RDS instance as specified in [RDS PostgreSQL](services/rds.md).\n   - Writes `/workspace/submission/manifest.json` (max 1 MiB) conforming strictly to `/workspace/contracts/schemas/manifest.schema.json`.\n   - Polls `GET <service_url>/health/ready` until the API returns HTTP `200`.\n   - Re-running `deploy.sh` must preserve the RDS instance, DynamoDB table, and S3 bucket without data loss, reconcile control-plane drift across all managed AWS resources (including SQS/DLQ attributes, EventBridge Scheduler schedules, CloudWatch Log Group retention, Lambda worker environment variables, out-of-band inline or attached IAM policies on the six workload roles, and security group egress rules), recreate any missing resources or event source mappings, and converge all derived data stores (DynamoDB, Valkey, and the versioned S3 audit bucket including purging noncurrent object `Versions` and `DeleteMarkers`) 1-to-1 with authoritative PostgreSQL state before exiting `0`.\n2. `/workspace/submission/destroy.sh`:\n   - Must be executable (timeout: 900s) and cleanly destroy all resources created for `resource_prefix` while leaving all pre-existing baseline (`cl-base-*`) resources untouched.\n   - Must handle non-empty versioned S3 buckets, attached or multi-version IAM policies, and any out-of-band resources scoped to `<resource_prefix>` (by name prefix or `ClearLedgerDeployment = <resource_prefix>` tag) so that post-destroy inventory matches the pre-deployment baseline.\n\n=== services/alb.md\n# Application Load Balancer (`services/alb.md`)\n\n- Provision an internet-facing Application Load Balancer (`aws_lb`, `load_balancer_type = \"application\"`, `internal = false`) attached to the two public subnets and the `alb` security group.\n- Provision an HTTP target group (`aws_lb_target_group`) on port `8080` (`protocol = \"HTTP\"`, `target_type = \"ip\"`) with a health check configured for:\n  - `path = \"/health/ready\"`\n  - `protocol = \"HTTP\"`\n  - `matcher = \"200\"`\n- Provision an HTTP listener (`aws_lb_listener`) on port `80` (`protocol = \"HTTP\"`) with a default `forward` action targeting the target group.\n- Expose `http://aws:80` (or `http://<alb_dns_name>`) as `service_url` in `manifest.json`.\n=== services/cloudwatch-logs.md\n# CloudWatch Log Groups (`services/cloudwatch-logs.md`)\n\nProvision four dedicated CloudWatch Log Groups (`aws_cloudwatch_log_group`), each with `retention_in_days = 14` (at least `14` days):\n\n- `logs.api_log_group`: `/clearledger/<resource_prefix>/api`\n- `logs.projector_log_group`: `/clearledger/<resource_prefix>/projector`\n- `logs.relay_log_group`: `/clearledger/<resource_prefix>/outbox-relay`\n- `logs.archiver_log_group`: `/clearledger/<resource_prefix>/audit-archiver`\n=== services/cognito.md\n# Cognito User Pool and OAuth2 Scopes (`services/cognito.md`)\n\n- Provision a Cognito User Pool (`aws_cognito_user_pool`) (an `aws_cognito_user_pool_domain` is optional).\n- Provision a Cognito Resource Server (`aws_cognito_resource_server`) with:\n  - `identifier = \"clearledger\"` (`manifest.auth.resource_server_identifier = \"clearledger\"`)\n  - Three scopes: `read`, `write`, and `admin` (yielding full scope strings `clearledger/read`, `clearledger/write`, and `clearledger/admin`).\n- Provision three separate Cognito User Pool Clients (`aws_cognito_user_pool_client`), each configured with:\n  - `generate_secret = true`\n  - `allowed_oauth_flows_user_pool_client = true`\n  - `allowed_oauth_flows = [\"client_credentials\"]`\n  - Exact single scope per client:\n    - `clients.read`: `allowed_oauth_scopes = [\"clearledger/read\"]`\n    - `clients.write`: `allowed_oauth_scopes = [\"clearledger/write\"]`\n    - `clients.admin`: `allowed_oauth_scopes = [\"clearledger/admin\"]`\n- Export `issuer_url` (`http://aws:4566/<user_pool_id>`) and `token_endpoint` (`http://aws:4566/cognito-idp/oauth2/token` or `http://aws:4566/<domain>.auth.<region>.amazoncognito.com/oauth2/token`) in `manifest.json`.\n=== services/dynamodb.md\n# DynamoDB Projection Store (`services/dynamodb.md`)\n\nProvision a DynamoDB table (`aws_dynamodb_table`) for settlement projections and ordered ledger events:\n\n- `billing_mode = \"PAY_PER_REQUEST\"`\n- Primary key:\n  - Partition key (`hash_key`): `PK` (`S`)\n  - Sort key (`range_key`): `SK` (`S`)\n- Global Secondary Index (`global_secondary_index`):\n  - `name = \"AccountIndex\"` (`manifest.projections.gsi_name = \"AccountIndex\"`)\n  - `hash_key = \"GSI1PK\"` (`S`)\n  - `range_key = \"GSI1SK\"` (`S`)\n  - `projection_type = \"ALL\"`\n- Point-in-time recovery (`point_in_time_recovery`):\n  - `enabled = true`\n- Server-side encryption (`server_side_encryption`):\n  - `enabled = true`\n  - `kms_key_arn = kms.projection_arn`\n\n## Projection Item Structure and Reconciliation Invariants\n\nFor each settlement `<settlement_id>`, the table stores:\n- One aggregate state item with `PK = \"SETTLEMENT#<settlement_id>\"`, `SK = \"STATE\"`, `GSI1PK = \"ACCOUNT#<account_id>\"`, `GSI1SK = \"SETTLEMENT#<settlement_id>\"`, and attributes `settlement_id`, `account_id`, `reference`, `debit_party`, `credit_party`, `status`, `clearing_stage`, `version` (`N`), `entry_count` (`N - 1`), `updated_at` (RFC3339 timestamp of the latest event), and optional `last_entry_id` / `last_memo`.\n- Ordered ledger event items with `PK = \"SETTLEMENT#<settlement_id>\"` and `SK = \"EVENT#<8-digit-zero-padded-version>\"` (`EVENT#00000001` .. `EVENT#<version:08d>`) containing `settlement_id`, `event_id`, `version` (`N`), `event_type`, `status`, `clearing_stage`, `occurred_at`, `correlation_id`, `envelope`, and optional `entry_id` / `memo`.\n\nBecause `clearledger-projector` writes `EVENT#*` items conditionally with `attribute_not_exists(PK) AND attribute_not_exists(SK)` and updates `STATE` conditionally only when `aggregateVersion > current_version`, `deploy.sh` must ensure full 1-to-1 convergence between PostgreSQL (`clearledger.settlements` and `clearledger.events`) and DynamoDB/Valkey so that no orphan partitions, stray sort keys, missing event items, or stale/divergent attributes remain in either DynamoDB or Valkey.\n\n=== services/ecs.md\n# ECS Fargate Service (`services/ecs.md`)\n\n- Provision an ECS cluster (`aws_ecs_cluster`) with Container Insights enabled (`setting { name = \"containerInsights\", value = \"enabled\" }`).\n- Provision an ECS task definition (`aws_ecs_task_definition`) with:\n  - `requires_compatibilities = [\"FARGATE\"]`\n  - `network_mode = \"awsvpc\"`\n  - `execution_role_arn` set to the `ecs_execution` IAM role\n  - `task_role_arn` set to the `ecs_task` IAM role\n  - Container definition named `api` running `api_image` from `config.json`, exposing TCP container port `8080`, configuring `logConfiguration` with `logDriver = \"awslogs\"` (`awslogs-group = logs.api_log_group`, `awslogs-region = <region>`), and defining all required environment variables from `runtime.md` (including `CACHE_TTL_SECONDS = \"90\"`).\n- Provision an ECS service (`aws_ecs_service`) with:\n  - `launch_type = \"FARGATE\"`\n  - `desired_count = 2` (at least 2 running tasks distributed across `us-east-1a` and `us-east-1b`)\n  - `network_configuration` attached strictly to the two private subnets (`network.private_subnet_ids`), the `ecs` security group, and `assign_public_ip = false`\n  - `load_balancer` block registering container `api` on port `8080` with the ALB target group\n\n=== services/elasticache-valkey.md\n# ElastiCache for Valkey (`services/elasticache-valkey.md`)\n\n- Provision an ElastiCache subnet group (`aws_elasticache_subnet_group`) spanning the private subnets.\n- Provision a Valkey 8 cache using `aws_elasticache_replication_group` (or `aws_elasticache_cluster`) with:\n  - `replication_group_id` (or `cluster_id`) prefixed with `<resource_prefix>`\n  - `engine = \"valkey\"` and `engine_version = \"8.0\"` (`engine = \"redis\"` is also accepted for provider compatibility)\n  - `node_type = \"cache.t4g.micro\"`\n  - `num_cache_clusters = 1` (or `num_cache_nodes = 1`)\n  - `port = 6379`\n  - `subnet_group_name` referencing the ElastiCache subnet group\n  - `security_group_ids` containing the `valkey` security group\n  - In-transit and at-rest encryption (`transit_encryption_enabled`, `at_rest_encryption_enabled`) are not required; cache connections use plain `redis://<endpoint>:<port>` inside the private VPC.\n- Pass `redis://<endpoint>:<port>` as `VALKEY_URL` to both the API service and the Projector Lambda, and set `CACHE_TTL_SECONDS = \"90\"` on the API container.\n=== services/iam.md\n# IAM Roles and Least-Privilege Policies (`services/iam.md`)\n\nProvision six distinct IAM roles (`aws_iam_role`) with role-specific inline or attached policies (`aws_iam_role_policy` or `aws_iam_policy` + `aws_iam_role_policy_attachment`).\n\n## Global Least-Privilege Rules\n\n- Never use wildcard actions (`Action = \"*\"` or `service:*`) or wildcard resources (`Resource = \"*\"`) in any `Allow` statement across any role policy.\n- **Per-workload CloudWatch Log Group isolation**: Each logging workload (`ecs_execution`, `ecs_task`, `projector`, `relay`, `archiver`) may only grant CloudWatch Logs write permissions (`logs:CreateLogStream`, `logs:PutLogEvents`, `logs:DescribeLogStreams`) on its **own** dedicated CloudWatch Log Group ARN (`arn:aws:logs:<region>:<account>:log-group:/clearledger/<resource_prefix>/<workload>*`). Do not use a shared `/clearledger/<resource_prefix>/*` or `/clearledger/*` log group wildcard that permits one workload to write into another workload's log group, and do not grant any CloudWatch Logs actions to `scheduler_role_arn`.\n- **Strict KMS key isolation across all six roles**: Each workload may only be granted `kms:Decrypt`, `kms:GenerateDataKey`, and `kms:DescribeKey` on the specific customer-managed KMS keys required for the data stores it accesses at runtime. No role may grant `kms:Decrypt` or `kms:GenerateDataKey` on any of the other KMS keys, and `ecs_execution_role_arn` and `scheduler_role_arn` must not grant access to any of the four KMS keys.\n- **Defense-in-depth explicit `Deny` guardrail statements**: Do not rely solely on implicit omission for forbidden permissions. Every role's policy document must include explicit `Effect = \"Deny\"` statement(s) covering all forbidden actions and resources listed under **Forbidden access (`Effect = \"Deny\"` required)** for that role below.\n- **Out-of-band IAM policy drift reconciliation in `deploy.sh`**: During `deploy.sh`, any out-of-band inline policies (`DeleteRolePolicy`) or attached customer-managed/AWS-managed policies (`DetachRolePolicy`) added to any of the six ClearLedger IAM roles outside Terraform/OpenTofu must be removed so that each role retains only its canonical least-privilege policy.\n\n## Role Responsibilities and Access Boundaries\n\n1. **`ecs_execution_role_arn`**:\n   - **Trust principal**: `ecs-tasks.amazonaws.com` (and no other service or wildcard principal).\n   - **Allowed access (`Effect = \"Allow\"`)**: CloudWatch Logs stream creation/writing (`logs:CreateLogStream`, `logs:PutLogEvents`, `logs:DescribeLogStreams`) scoped strictly to `logs.api_log_group`.\n   - **Forbidden access (`Effect = \"Deny\"` required)**: Must not allow, and must explicitly deny via `Effect = \"Deny\"`, any SQS actions (`sqs:SendMessage`, `sqs:ReceiveMessage`, `sqs:DeleteMessage`) on the main queue or DLQ, any DynamoDB actions (`dynamodb:GetItem`, `dynamodb:PutItem`, `dynamodb:UpdateItem`, `dynamodb:DeleteItem`, `dynamodb:Query`) on the projection table or `AccountIndex`, any S3 actions (`s3:GetObject`, `s3:PutObject`, `s3:DeleteObject`, `s3:ListBucket`) on the audit bucket (`<audit_bucket_arn>` and `<audit_bucket_arn>/*`), `kms:Decrypt` or `kms:GenerateDataKey` on all four KMS keys (`database`, `messaging`, `projection`, `audit`), and `logs:CreateLogStream` or `logs:PutLogEvents` on the three non-API CloudWatch Log Groups (`projector`, `relay`, `archiver`).\n2. **`ecs_task_role_arn`**:\n   - **Trust principal**: `ecs-tasks.amazonaws.com`.\n   - **Allowed access (`Effect = \"Allow\"`)**:\n     - Publish-only access on the main SQS queue ARN (`sqs:SendMessage`, `sqs:GetQueueAttributes`, `sqs:GetQueueUrl`).\n     - Read-only access on the projection DynamoDB table ARN and its specific `AccountIndex` GSI ARN `<table_arn>/index/AccountIndex` (`dynamodb:GetItem`, `dynamodb:Query`, `dynamodb:DescribeTable`; do not use `<table_arn>/index/*` or `<table_arn>/*` wildcards).\n     - Cryptographic usage (`kms:Decrypt`, `kms:GenerateDataKey`, `kms:DescribeKey`) on the `messaging` and `projection` KMS key ARNs only.\n     - CloudWatch Logs stream creation/writing scoped strictly to `logs.api_log_group`.\n   - **Forbidden access (`Effect = \"Deny\"` required)**: Must not allow, and must explicitly deny via `Effect = \"Deny\"`, consuming or deleting SQS messages (`sqs:ReceiveMessage`, `sqs:DeleteMessage`) on the main queue or `sqs:SendMessage`/`sqs:ReceiveMessage`/`sqs:DeleteMessage` on the DLQ; mutating DynamoDB (`dynamodb:PutItem`, `dynamodb:UpdateItem`, `dynamodb:DeleteItem`, `dynamodb:DeleteTable`) on the projection table; any S3 access (`s3:GetObject`, `s3:PutObject`, `s3:DeleteObject`, `s3:ListBucket`) on `<audit_bucket_arn>` and `<audit_bucket_arn>/*`; `kms:Decrypt` or `kms:GenerateDataKey` on the `database` and `audit` KMS keys; and `logs:CreateLogStream` or `logs:PutLogEvents` on any non-API log group (`projector`, `relay`, `archiver`). Furthermore, must not grant `dynamodb:Query` on `<table_arn>/index/*`.\n3. **`projector_role_arn`**:\n   - **Trust principal**: `lambda.amazonaws.com`.\n   - **Allowed access (`Effect = \"Allow\"`)**:\n     - Consume-only access on the main SQS queue ARN (`sqs:ReceiveMessage`, `sqs:DeleteMessage`, `sqs:GetQueueAttributes`, `sqs:ChangeMessageVisibility`) and dead-letter forwarding (`sqs:SendMessage`) on the DLQ ARN.\n     - Append/monotonic-upsert access on the projection DynamoDB table ARN and its specific `AccountIndex` GSI ARN `<table_arn>/index/AccountIndex` (`dynamodb:GetItem`, `dynamodb:PutItem`, `dynamodb:UpdateItem`, `dynamodb:Query`; do not use `<table_arn>/index/*` or `<table_arn>/*` wildcards).\n     - Cryptographic usage (`kms:Decrypt`, `kms:GenerateDataKey`, `kms:DescribeKey`) on the `messaging` and `projection` KMS key ARNs only.\n     - CloudWatch Logs stream creation/writing scoped strictly to `logs.projector_log_group`.\n   - **Forbidden access (`Effect = \"Deny\"` required)**: Must not allow, and must explicitly deny via `Effect = \"Deny\"`, `sqs:SendMessage` on the main queue or `sqs:ReceiveMessage`/`sqs:DeleteMessage` on the DLQ; deleting DynamoDB items (`dynamodb:DeleteItem`) or tables (`dynamodb:DeleteTable`) on the projection table; any S3 access (`s3:GetObject`, `s3:PutObject`, `s3:DeleteObject`, `s3:ListBucket`) on `<audit_bucket_arn>` and `<audit_bucket_arn>/*`; `kms:Decrypt` or `kms:GenerateDataKey` on the `database` and `audit` KMS keys; and `logs:CreateLogStream` or `logs:PutLogEvents` on any non-projector log group (`api`, `relay`, `archiver`). Furthermore, must not grant `dynamodb:Query` on `<table_arn>/index/*`.\n4. **`relay_role_arn`**:\n   - **Trust principal**: `lambda.amazonaws.com`.\n   - **Allowed access (`Effect = \"Allow\"`)**:\n     - Publish-only access on the main SQS queue ARN (`sqs:SendMessage`, `sqs:GetQueueAttributes`, `sqs:GetQueueUrl`).\n     - Cryptographic usage (`kms:Decrypt`, `kms:GenerateDataKey`, `kms:DescribeKey`) on the `messaging` and `database` KMS key ARNs only.\n     - CloudWatch Logs stream creation/writing scoped strictly to `logs.relay_log_group`.\n   - **Forbidden access (`Effect = \"Deny\"` required)**: Must not allow, and must explicitly deny via `Effect = \"Deny\"`, `sqs:ReceiveMessage` or `sqs:DeleteMessage` on the main queue, `sqs:SendMessage`/`sqs:ReceiveMessage`/`sqs:DeleteMessage` on the DLQ, any DynamoDB actions (`dynamodb:GetItem`, `dynamodb:PutItem`, `dynamodb:UpdateItem`, `dynamodb:DeleteItem`, `dynamodb:Query`) on the projection table or `AccountIndex`, any S3 actions (`s3:GetObject`, `s3:PutObject`, `s3:DeleteObject`, `s3:ListBucket`) on `<audit_bucket_arn>` and `<audit_bucket_arn>/*`, `kms:Decrypt` or `kms:GenerateDataKey` on the `projection` and `audit` KMS keys, and `logs:CreateLogStream` or `logs:PutLogEvents` on any non-relay log group (`api`, `projector`, `archiver`).\n5. **`archiver_role_arn`**:\n   - **Trust principal**: `lambda.amazonaws.com`.\n   - **Allowed access (`Effect = \"Allow\"`)**:\n     - Append-only object access (`s3:PutObject`, `s3:GetObject`, `s3:AbortMultipartUpload`) scoped strictly to `<audit_bucket_arn>/ledger-audit/*`, and bucket-level metadata (`s3:ListBucket`, `s3:GetBucketLocation`) scoped strictly to `<audit_bucket_arn>` (keep object-level and bucket-level statements separated by resource type).\n     - Cryptographic usage (`kms:Decrypt`, `kms:GenerateDataKey`, `kms:DescribeKey`) on the `audit` and `database` KMS key ARNs only.\n     - CloudWatch Logs stream creation/writing scoped strictly to `logs.archiver_log_group`.\n   - **Forbidden access (`Effect = \"Deny\"` required)**: Must not grant `s3:PutObject` on the bucket ARN `<audit_bucket_arn>` or unscoped `<audit_bucket_arn>/*` root wildcard, and must not grant `s3:ListBucket` on object ARNs. Must explicitly deny via `Effect = \"Deny\"` `s3:DeleteObject` and `s3:DeleteObjectVersion` on `<audit_bucket_arn>` and `<audit_bucket_arn>/*`, any SQS actions (`sqs:SendMessage`, `sqs:ReceiveMessage`, `sqs:DeleteMessage`) on the main queue or DLQ, any DynamoDB actions (`dynamodb:GetItem`, `dynamodb:PutItem`, `dynamodb:UpdateItem`, `dynamodb:DeleteItem`, `dynamodb:Query`) on the projection table or `AccountIndex`, `kms:Decrypt` or `kms:GenerateDataKey` on the `messaging` and `projection` KMS keys, and `logs:CreateLogStream` or `logs:PutLogEvents` on any non-archiver log group (`api`, `projector`, `relay`).\n6. **`scheduler_role_arn`**:\n   - **Trust principal**: `scheduler.amazonaws.com`.\n   - **Allowed access (`Effect = \"Allow\"`)**:\n     - `lambda:InvokeFunction` scoped strictly to the Outbox Relay (`workers.outbox_relay.function_arn`) and Audit Archiver (`workers.audit_archiver.function_arn`) Lambda function ARNs.\n   - **Forbidden access (`Effect = \"Deny\"` required)**: Must not allow, and must explicitly deny via `Effect = \"Deny\"`, `lambda:InvokeFunction` on the Projector Lambda (`workers.projector.function_arn`), any SQS actions (`sqs:SendMessage`, `sqs:ReceiveMessage`, `sqs:DeleteMessage`) on the main queue or DLQ, any DynamoDB actions (`dynamodb:GetItem`, `dynamodb:PutItem`, `dynamodb:UpdateItem`, `dynamodb:DeleteItem`, `dynamodb:Query`) on the projection table or `AccountIndex`, any S3 actions (`s3:GetObject`, `s3:PutObject`, `s3:DeleteObject`, `s3:ListBucket`) on `<audit_bucket_arn>` and `<audit_bucket_arn>/*`, `kms:Decrypt` or `kms:GenerateDataKey` on all four KMS keys (`database`, `messaging`, `projection`, `audit`), and `logs:CreateLogStream` or `logs:PutLogEvents` on all four CloudWatch Log Groups (`api`, `projector`, `relay`, `archiver`).\n=== services/kms.md\n# Customer-Managed KMS Keys (`services/kms.md`)\n\nProvision four separate customer-managed KMS keys (`aws_kms_key`) and four KMS aliases (`aws_kms_alias`):\n\n- `kms.database_arn` with alias `alias/<resource_prefix>-database`: encrypts the RDS PostgreSQL instance\n- `kms.messaging_arn` with alias `alias/<resource_prefix>-messaging`: encrypts the main SQS queue and DLQ\n- `kms.projection_arn` with alias `alias/<resource_prefix>-projection`: encrypts the DynamoDB projection table\n- `kms.audit_arn` with alias `alias/<resource_prefix>-audit`: encrypts the S3 audit archive bucket\n\nEach of the four KMS keys must be configured with:\n- `enable_key_rotation = true`\n- `deletion_window_in_days = 10` (must be `10..30` days)\n- Tag `ClearLedgerDeployment = <resource_prefix>`\n\n=== services/lambda.md\n# Lambda Workers and Event Source Mapping (`services/lambda.md`)\n\nProvision three container-based AWS Lambda functions (`aws_lambda_function`, `package_type = \"Image\"`):\n\n1. **Projector (`workers.projector`)**:\n   - `image_uri = projector_image` from `config.json`\n   - `role = iam.projector_role_arn`\n   - Environment variables: `AWS_ENDPOINT_URL`, `PROJECTION_TABLE`, `VALKEY_URL`, `CLOUDWATCH_LOG_GROUP`\n   - Wired to the main SQS queue via an `aws_lambda_event_source_mapping` with:\n     - `enabled = true`\n     - `batch_size = 5`\n     - `maximum_batching_window_in_seconds = 0`\n     - `function_response_types = [\"ReportBatchItemFailures\"]`\n2. **Outbox Relay (`workers.outbox_relay`)**:\n   - `image_uri = relay_image` from `config.json`\n   - `role = iam.relay_role_arn`\n   - Environment variables: `AWS_ENDPOINT_URL`, `DATABASE_URL`, `SQS_QUEUE_URL`, `OUTBOX_BATCH_SIZE = \"50\"`, `CLOUDWATCH_LOG_GROUP`\n3. **Audit Archiver (`workers.audit_archiver`)**:\n   - `image_uri = archiver_image` from `config.json`\n   - `role = iam.archiver_role_arn`\n   - Environment variables: `AWS_ENDPOINT_URL`, `DATABASE_URL`, `AUDIT_BUCKET`, `AUDIT_PREFIX = \"ledger-audit/\"`, `CLOUDWATCH_LOG_GROUP`\n=== services/rds.md\n# RDS PostgreSQL (`services/rds.md`)\n\n- Provision a DB subnet group (`aws_db_subnet_group`) spanning strictly the private subnets (`network.private_subnet_ids`).\n- Provision a single PostgreSQL 16 RDS instance (`aws_db_instance`) with:\n  - `engine = \"postgres\"`\n  - `engine_version = \"16.3\"` (or `16.*`)\n  - `instance_class = \"db.t4g.micro\"`\n  - `db_name`, `username`, and `password` from `/workspace/config/config.json`\n  - `storage_encrypted = true`\n  - `kms_key_id` set to the customer-managed `database` KMS key ARN (`kms.database_arn`)\n  - `publicly_accessible = false`\n  - `skip_final_snapshot = true`\n  - `vpc_security_group_ids` containing the `rds` security group\n- Re-running `deploy.sh` must never replace the RDS instance or lose committed data.\n\n## Required Database Schema, Relational Invariants, and Triggers\n\nThe pre-built Rust binaries (`clearledger-api`, `clearledger-outbox-relay`, and `clearledger-audit-archiver`) do **not** run database migrations on startup. `clearledger-api` relies on PostgreSQL `CHECK`/`UNIQUE`/`FOREIGN KEY` constraints and PL/pgSQL triggers to enforce domain, schema, and state-transition invariants at the database layer (mapping SQLSTATE `23514`, `23503`, `23502`, `23505`, and `P0001` exceptions to HTTP `400 Bad Request`), and `GET /health/ready` verifies that all four tables, all three indexes, and user-defined triggers on all four tables exist in the `clearledger` schema before reporting `checks.postgres = \"UP\"`.\n\n`deploy.sh` must idempotently initialize the `clearledger` schema, all four tables, their constraints, their triggers, and all three indexes on the RDS PostgreSQL instance.\n\n### 1. Table `clearledger.settlements`\n\n- **Columns**:\n  - `settlement_id UUID NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY`\n  - `account_id TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `reference TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `debit_party TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `credit_party TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `current_status TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `current_stage TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `last_entry_id UUID NULL`\n  - `last_memo TEXT NULL`\n  - `version INTEGER NOT NULL`\n  - `entry_count INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0`\n  - `created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()`\n  - `updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()`\n- **Required invariants (enforced via `CHECK` constraints and `BEFORE UPDATE` trigger)**:\n  - Trimmed string length bounds and allowed status values must match `CreateSettlementRequest` and `AppendEntryRequest` in `openapi.yaml` (including `1..256` trimmed characters when `last_memo` is non-null), with trimmed `debit_party <> credit_party`, `version >= 1`, and monotonic timestamps (`updated_at >= created_at` on insert and `NEW.updated_at >= OLD.updated_at` on update).\n  - At initiation (`version = 1`), `entry_count = 0`, `current_status = 'INITIATED'`, `last_entry_id IS NULL`, and `updated_at = created_at`. For `version > 1`, `entry_count = version - 1`, `current_status <> 'INITIATED'`, and `last_entry_id IS NOT NULL`.\n  - On `UPDATE`: header columns (`settlement_id`, `account_id`, `reference`, `debit_party`, `credit_party`, `created_at`) are immutable; each update must step `version` and `entry_count` by `+1` with a new `last_entry_id` (`NEW.last_entry_id IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.last_entry_id`); and `current_status` must follow the clearing lifecycle progression:\n    - The non-`DISPUTED` clearing status rank order is `INITIATED` (`0`) < `VALIDATED` (`1`) < `RESERVED` (`2`) < `CLEARED` (`3`) < `SETTLED` (`4`) < `RECONCILED` (`5`).\n    - `RECONCILED` is strictly terminal: once `OLD.current_status = 'RECONCILED'`, any `UPDATE` on the settlement row must be rejected (even if `NEW.current_status = 'RECONCILED'`).\n    - From any non-`RECONCILED`, non-`DISPUTED` status (`INITIATED`, `VALIDATED`, `RESERVED`, `CLEARED`, `SETTLED`), `NEW.current_status` may transition to `'DISPUTED'` or to any status whose rank is monotonic non-decreasing (`rank(NEW.current_status) >= rank(OLD.current_status)` — permitting same-status updates such as `CLEARED` -> `CLEARED` and single- or multi-stage forward advances such as `INITIATED` -> `VALIDATED` or `INITIATED` -> `CLEARED`, while rejecting any backward rank regression where `rank(NEW.current_status) < rank(OLD.current_status)` such as `CLEARED` -> `RESERVED`).\n    - From `OLD.current_status = 'DISPUTED'`, `NEW.current_status` may only remain `'DISPUTED'` or resolve to terminal `'RECONCILED'`.\n\n### 2. Table `clearledger.events`\n\n- **Columns**:\n  - `seq BIGSERIAL NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY`\n  - `event_id UUID NOT NULL UNIQUE`\n  - `settlement_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES clearledger.settlements(settlement_id) ON DELETE CASCADE`\n  - `aggregate_version INTEGER NOT NULL`\n  - `event_type TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `correlation_id TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `idempotency_key TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `occurred_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL`\n  - `payload JSONB NOT NULL`\n  - `created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()`\n- **Required invariants (enforced via `UNIQUE`/`CHECK` constraints and `BEFORE INSERT`/`UPDATE`/`DELETE` triggers)**:\n  - Composite `UNIQUE (settlement_id, aggregate_version)` and `UNIQUE (settlement_id, idempotency_key)`, with trimmed length bounds on `correlation_id` and `idempotency_key` matching `openapi.yaml`.\n  - `payload` must conform strictly to `ClearLedgerDomainEventEnvelope` in `schemas/events.schema.json` (including `additionalProperties: false` on both the envelope and `payload.data`, required non-null `accountId`, `reference`, `debitParty`, `creditParty`, `status`, and `clearingStage` in `payload.data` with trimmed string length bounds matching `openapi.yaml` and `1..256` trimmed characters when `data.memo` is non-null, column-to-envelope equality across `event_id`, `settlement_id`, `aggregate_version`, `event_type`, `correlation_id`, `idempotency_key`, and `occurred_at` compared as `TIMESTAMPTZ`, and version/kind/status/entryId coupling for `SettlementInitiated` at `v = 1` vs `LedgerEntryRecorded` at `v >= 2`).\n  - On `INSERT`: per-settlement `aggregate_version` must be contiguous starting at `1`; `data.entryId` must be unique per settlement across all `LedgerEntryRecorded` events; the event must match the parent row in `clearledger.settlements` (including header fields, `current_status`, `current_stage`, `last_entry_id`, `last_memo`, `version`, and `occurred_at = updated_at`, plus `occurred_at = created_at` at `v = 1`); and for `aggregate_version >= 2`, `NEW.occurred_at` must be `>=` the immediately preceding event's `occurred_at` (`aggregate_version = NEW.aggregate_version - 1`) and the status transition from that preceding event's `data.status` to `NEW.payload->'data'->>'status'` must satisfy the settlement status transition rules above (monotonic non-decreasing rank, reachable `DISPUTED` from non-`RECONCILED`, `DISPUTED` only to `DISPUTED` or `RECONCILED`, and no transitions out of or repeating `RECONCILED`).\n  - `clearledger.events` is strictly append-only (`UPDATE` and `DELETE` must be rejected).\n\n### 3. Table `clearledger.outbox`\n\n- **Columns**:\n  - `seq BIGSERIAL NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY`\n  - `event_id UUID NOT NULL UNIQUE REFERENCES clearledger.events(event_id) ON DELETE CASCADE`\n  - `settlement_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES clearledger.settlements(settlement_id) ON DELETE CASCADE`\n  - `aggregate_version INTEGER NOT NULL`\n  - `correlation_id TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `payload JSONB NOT NULL`\n  - `created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()`\n  - `published_at TIMESTAMPTZ NULL`\n  - `archived_at TIMESTAMPTZ NULL`\n  - `attempts INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0`\n  - `last_error TEXT NULL`\n- **Required invariants (enforced via `UNIQUE`/`FOREIGN KEY`/`CHECK` constraints and triggers)**:\n  - Composite `UNIQUE (settlement_id, aggregate_version)` and `FOREIGN KEY (settlement_id, aggregate_version) REFERENCES clearledger.events(settlement_id, aggregate_version) ON DELETE CASCADE`.\n  - `payload` must conform to `ClearLedgerDomainEventEnvelope` (`schemas/events.schema.json`) and on `INSERT` must exactly mirror the corresponding row in `clearledger.events` in contiguous per-settlement `aggregate_version` order starting at `1`.\n  - Delivery/archival lifecycle: `attempts >= 0`; when `published_at IS NOT NULL`, `attempts >= 1`, `last_error IS NULL`, and `published_at >= created_at`; `archived_at` requires `published_at IS NOT NULL` and `archived_at >= published_at`.\n  - `DELETE` is forbidden; on `UPDATE`, envelope columns are immutable, `attempts` cannot decrease, publishing an unpublished row requires incrementing `attempts` with `archived_at IS NULL`, while `published_at` remains `NOT NULL` (such as during archival or resetting `archived_at = NULL` for S3 re-archival), `published_at`, `attempts`, and `last_error` cannot be mutated (resetting `published_at = NULL, archived_at = NULL` for operational replay is permitted), and once `OLD.archived_at IS NOT NULL` and `NEW.archived_at IS NOT NULL`, `NEW.archived_at` cannot be mutated to a different timestamp without first resetting `archived_at = NULL`.\n\n### 4. Table `clearledger.idempotency_keys`\n\n- **Columns**:\n  - `scope TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `idempotency_key TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `request_hash TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `status_code INTEGER NOT NULL`\n  - `response_body JSONB NOT NULL`\n  - `created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()`\n  - Composite `PRIMARY KEY (scope, idempotency_key)`\n- **Required invariants (enforced via `CHECK` constraints and triggers)**:\n  - `scope` (`create:<settlement_uuid>` or `entry:<settlement_uuid>`), `idempotency_key` (`8..128` trimmed chars), lowercase hex SHA-256 `request_hash`, `status_code` (`201` for `create:*` at `version = 1`; `202` for `entry:*` at `version >= 2`), and closed-schema `response_body` (`WriteAcceptedResponse` in `openapi.yaml` with `accepted = true` and `idempotentReplay = false`).\n  - On `INSERT`, the referenced event (`eventId`, `settlementId`, `version`, `idempotency_key`) must exist in both `clearledger.events` and `clearledger.outbox`. `UPDATE` and `DELETE` must be rejected.\n\n### 5. Required Indexes in `clearledger` Schema\n\n- `idx_clearledger_outbox_unpublished`: on `clearledger.outbox (seq)` with partial predicate `WHERE published_at IS NULL`\n- `idx_clearledger_outbox_unarchived`: on `clearledger.outbox (seq)` with partial predicate `WHERE published_at IS NOT NULL AND archived_at IS NULL`\n- `idx_clearledger_events_settlement_version`: on `clearledger.events (settlement_id, aggregate_version)`\n=== services/s3.md\n# S3 Audit Archive (`services/s3.md`)\n\n- Provision a dedicated S3 bucket (`aws_s3_bucket`) for NDJSON audit batches (`force_destroy = true` recommended so `destroy.sh` can remove the bucket after versioned audit objects and delete markers are written).\n- Enable bucket versioning (`aws_s3_bucket_versioning` with `status = \"Enabled\"`).\n- Enable default server-side encryption (`aws_s3_bucket_server_side_encryption_configuration`) using `sse_algorithm = \"aws:kms\"` and `kms_master_key_id = kms.audit_arn`.\n- Block all public access (`aws_s3_bucket_public_access_block`) with all four flags (`block_public_acls`, `block_public_policy`, `ignore_public_acls`, `restrict_public_buckets`) set to `true`.\n- Configure the Audit Archiver Lambda with `AUDIT_BUCKET = <bucket_name>` and `AUDIT_PREFIX = \"ledger-audit/\"` (`manifest.audit.prefix = \"ledger-audit/\"`).\n- `clearledger-audit-archiver` writes NDJSON batches to `s3://<bucket_name>/ledger-audit/batch-<first_seq:08d>-<last_seq:08d>-<16_char_sha256_hex>.ndjson`, where `<first_seq:08d>` and `<last_seq:08d>` are the 8-digit zero-padded first and last `clearledger.outbox.seq` in the batch, the NDJSON lines inside each batch are ordered by strictly ascending `clearledger.outbox.seq`, and `<16_char_sha256_hex>` is the first 16 lowercase hexadecimal characters of the SHA-256 digest of the object's raw bytes.\n- During `deploy.sh` data-plane convergence, reconcile the versioned S3 audit bucket against `clearledger.outbox` so that all objects reside under `ledger-audit/`, every batch object has a valid canonical key (`batch-<first_seq:08d>-<last_seq:08d>-<16_char_sha256_hex>.ndjson`), valid SHA-256 digest, and strictly ascending `clearledger.outbox.seq` order, every committed event in `clearledger.outbox` appears **exactly once** across the S3 audit batches with its exact canonical JSON `payload` and `archived_at IS NOT NULL` in PostgreSQL, and all noncurrent object `Versions` (`IsLatest = false`) and `DeleteMarkers` (`ListObjectVersions` / `DeleteObject` with `VersionId`) are permanently purged from the bucket so only the single current version (`IsLatest = true`) of each valid canonical batch remains.\n\n=== services/scheduler.md\n# EventBridge Scheduler (`services/scheduler.md`)\n\nProvision two enabled EventBridge services/sqs.md\n# SQS Main Queue and Dead-Letter Queue (`services/sqs.md`)\n\n- Provision a dead-letter queue (`aws_sqs_queue`) encrypted with the customer-managed `messaging` KMS key (`kms_master_key_id = kms.messaging_arn`) and `message_retention_seconds = 1209600` (14 days).\n- Provision the main event queue (`aws_sqs_queue`) with:\n  - `kms_master_key_id = kms.messaging_arn`\n  - `visibility_timeout_seconds = 3`\n  - `receive_wait_time_seconds = 2`\n  - `message_retention_seconds = 172800` (2 days)\n  - Redrive policy (configured either inline via `redrive_policy` on `aws_sqs_queue` or via a standalone `aws_sqs_queue_redrive_policy` resource) routing failed messages to the DLQ with `maxReceiveCount = 4` (`manifest.messaging.max_receive_count = 4`).\n- If the main queue or its Lambda event source mapping is deleted during an outage drill, or if queue attributes (such as `VisibilityTimeout` on the main queue or `MessageRetentionPeriod` on the DLQ) drift out-of-band, re-running `deploy.sh` must reconcile or recreate the queue, re-bind its Lambda event source mapping cleanly, update `manifest.json`, and drain/reconcile any pending outbox events, true`) and DNS hostnames (`enable_dns_hostnames = true`) enabled.\n- Create at least two public subnets (`aws_subnet`, `map_public_ip_on_launch = true`) across `us-east-1a` and `us-east-1b`.\n- Create at least two private subnets (`aws_subnet`, `map_public_ip_on_launch = false`) across `us-east-1a` and `us-east-1b`.\n- Attach an Internet Gateway (`aws_internet_gateway`) to the VPC and associate (`aws_route_table_association`) a public route table (`0.0.0.0/0` -> Internet Gateway) with both public subnets.\n- Associate (`aws_route_table_association`) a private route table (with no route to an Internet Gateway) with both private subnets.\n- Create four distinct dedicated security groups (`aws_security_group`, four distinct security group IDs) enforcing least-privilege ingress and egress:\n  - `alb`:\n    - **Ingress**: TCP port `80` (`from_port = 80`, `to_port = 80`, `protocol = \"tcp\"`) from `0.0.0.0/0`.\n    - **Egress**: restricted strictly to TCP port `8080` (`from_port = 8080`, `to_port = 8080`, `protocol = \"tcp\"`) targeting the `ecs` security group or VPC CIDR (must **not** allow `0.0.0.0/0`, `::/0`, or `protocol = \"-1\"` on egress).\n  - `ecs`:\n    - **Ingress**: restricted strictly to TCP port `8080` (`from_port = 8080`, `to_port = 8080`, `protocol = \"tcp\"`) from the `alb` security group ID (never `0.0.0.0/0`, `::/0`, or any CIDR block).\n    - **Egress**: outbound traffic to the AWS control plane and data stores.\n  - `rds`:\n    - **Ingress**: restricted strictly to TCP port `5432` (`from_port = 5432`, `to_port = 5432`, `protocol = \"tcp\"`) from the `ecs` security group or VPC CIDR (never `0.0.0.0/0`, `::/0`, or `protocol = \"-1\"`).\n    - **Egress**: restricted to the VPC (must **not** allow `0.0.0.0/0` or `::/0` on egress).\n  - `valkey`:\n    - **Ingress**: restricted strictly to TCP port `6379` (`from_port = 6379`, `to_port = 6379`, `protocol = \"tcp\"`) from the `ecs` security group or VPC CIDR (never `0.0.0.0/0`, `::/0`, or `protocol = \"-1\"`).\n    - **Egress**: restricted to the VPC (must **not** allow `0.0.0.0/0` or `::/0` on egress).\n- Tag the VPC, subnets, internet gateway, route tables, and security groups with `ClearLedgerDeployment = <resource_prefix>`.\n\n","metadata":{"output":"...\n\naws_elasticache_replication_group` (or `aws_elasticache_cluster`) with:\n  - `replication_group_id` (or `cluster_id`) prefixed with `<resource_prefix>`\n  - `engine = \"valkey\"` and `engine_version = \"8.0\"` (`engine = \"redis\"` is also accepted for provider compatibility)\n  - `node_type = \"cache.t4g.micro\"`\n  - `num_cache_clusters = 1` (or `num_cache_nodes = 1`)\n  - `port = 6379`\n  - `subnet_group_name` referencing the ElastiCache subnet group\n  - `security_group_ids` containing the `valkey` security group\n  - In-transit and at-rest encryption (`transit_encryption_enabled`, `at_rest_encryption_enabled`) are not required; cache connections use plain `redis://<endpoint>:<port>` inside the private VPC.\n- Pass `redis://<endpoint>:<port>` as `VALKEY_URL` to both the API service and the Projector Lambda, and set `CACHE_TTL_SECONDS = \"90\"` on the API container.\n=== services/iam.md\n# IAM Roles and Least-Privilege Policies (`services/iam.md`)\n\nProvision six distinct IAM roles (`aws_iam_role`) with role-specific inline or attached policies (`aws_iam_role_policy` or `aws_iam_policy` + `aws_iam_role_policy_attachment`).\n\n## Global Least-Privilege Rules\n\n- Never use wildcard actions (`Action = \"*\"` or `service:*`) or wildcard resources (`Resource = \"*\"`) in any `Allow` statement across any role policy.\n- **Per-workload CloudWatch Log Group isolation**: Each logging workload (`ecs_execution`, `ecs_task`, `projector`, `relay`, `archiver`) may only grant CloudWatch Logs write permissions (`logs:CreateLogStream`, `logs:PutLogEvents`, `logs:DescribeLogStreams`) on its **own** dedicated CloudWatch Log Group ARN (`arn:aws:logs:<region>:<account>:log-group:/clearledger/<resource_prefix>/<workload>*`). Do not use a shared `/clearledger/<resource_prefix>/*` or `/clearledger/*` log group wildcard that permits one workload to write into another workload's log group, and do not grant any CloudWatch Logs actions to `scheduler_role_arn`.\n- **Strict KMS key isolation across all six roles**: Each workload may only be granted `kms:Decrypt`, `kms:GenerateDataKey`, and `kms:DescribeKey` on the specific customer-managed KMS keys required for the data stores it accesses at runtime. No role may grant `kms:Decrypt` or `kms:GenerateDataKey` on any of the other KMS keys, and `ecs_execution_role_arn` and `scheduler_role_arn` must not grant access to any of the four KMS keys.\n- **Defense-in-depth explicit `Deny` guardrail statements**: Do not rely solely on implicit omission for forbidden permissions. Every role's policy document must include explicit `Effect = \"Deny\"` statement(s) covering all forbidden actions and resources listed under **Forbidden access (`Effect = \"Deny\"` required)** for that role below.\n- **Out-of-band IAM policy drift reconciliation in `deploy.sh`**: During `deploy.sh`, any out-of-band inline policies (`DeleteRolePolicy`) or attached customer-managed/AWS-managed policies (`DetachRolePolicy`) added to any of the six ClearLedger IAM roles outside Terraform/OpenTofu must be removed so that each role retains only its canonical least-privilege policy.\n\n## Role Responsibilities and Access Boundaries\n\n1. **`ecs_execution_role_arn`**:\n   - **Trust principal**: `ecs-tasks.amazonaws.com` (and no other service or wildcard principal).\n   - **Allowed access (`Effect = \"Allow\"`)**: CloudWatch Logs stream creation/writing (`logs:CreateLogStream`, `logs:PutLogEvents`, `logs:DescribeLogStreams`) scoped strictly to `logs.api_log_group`.\n   - **Forbidden access (`Effect = \"Deny\"` required)**: Must not allow, and must explicitly deny via `Effect = \"Deny\"`, any SQS actions (`sqs:SendMessage`, `sqs:ReceiveMessage`, `sqs:DeleteMessage`) on the main queue or DLQ, any DynamoDB actions (`dynamodb:GetItem`, `dynamodb:PutItem`, `dynamodb:UpdateItem`, `dynamodb:DeleteItem`, `dynamodb:Query`) on the projection table or `AccountIndex`, any S3 actions (`s3:GetObject`, `s3:PutObject`, `s3:DeleteObject`, `s3:ListBucket`) on the audit bucket (`<audit_bucket_arn>` and `<audit_bucket_arn>/*`), `kms:Decrypt` or `kms:GenerateDataKey` on all four KMS keys (`database`, `messaging`, `projection`, `audit`), and `logs:CreateLogStream` or `logs:PutLogEvents` on the three non-API CloudWatch Log Groups (`projector`, `relay`, `archiver`).\n2. **`ecs_task_role_arn`**:\n   - **Trust principal**: `ecs-tasks.amazonaws.com`.\n   - **Allowed access (`Effect = \"Allow\"`)**:\n     - Publish-only access on the main SQS queue ARN (`sqs:SendMessage`, `sqs:GetQueueAttributes`, `sqs:GetQueueUrl`).\n     - Read-only access on the projection DynamoDB table ARN and its specific `AccountIndex` GSI ARN `<table_arn>/index/AccountIndex` (`dynamodb:GetItem`, `dynamodb:Query`, `dynamodb:DescribeTable`; do not use `<table_arn>/index/*` or `<table_arn>/*` wildcards).\n     - Cryptographic usage (`kms:Decrypt`, `kms:GenerateDataKey`, `kms:DescribeKey`) on the `messaging` and `projection` KMS key ARNs only.\n     - CloudWatch Logs stream creation/writing scoped strictly to `logs.api_log_group`.\n   - **Forbidden access (`Effect = \"Deny\"` required)**: Must not allow, and must explicitly deny via `Effect = \"Deny\"`, consuming or deleting SQS messages (`sqs:ReceiveMessage`, `sqs:DeleteMessage`) on the main queue or `sqs:SendMessage`/`sqs:ReceiveMessage`/`sqs:DeleteMessage` on the DLQ; mutating DynamoDB (`dynamodb:PutItem`, `dynamodb:UpdateItem`, `dynamodb:DeleteItem`, `dynamodb:DeleteTable`) on the projection table; any S3 access (`s3:GetObject`, `s3:PutObject`, `s3:DeleteObject`, `s3:ListBucket`) on `<audit_bucket_arn>` and `<audit_bucket_arn>/*`; `kms:Decrypt` or `kms:GenerateDataKey` on the `database` and `audit` KMS keys; and `logs:CreateLogStream` or `logs:PutLogEvents` on any non-API log group (`projector`, `relay`, `archiver`). Furthermore, must not grant `dynamodb:Query` on `<table_arn>/index/*`.\n3. **`projector_role_arn`**:\n   - **Trust principal**: `lambda.amazonaws.com`.\n   - **Allowed access (`Effect = \"Allow\"`)**:\n     - Consume-only access on the main SQS queue ARN (`sqs:ReceiveMessage`, `sqs:DeleteMessage`, `sqs:GetQueueAttributes`, `sqs:ChangeMessageVisibility`) and dead-letter forwarding (`sqs:SendMessage`) on the DLQ ARN.\n     - Append/monotonic-upsert access on the projection DynamoDB table ARN and its specific `AccountIndex` GSI ARN `<table_arn>/index/AccountIndex` (`dynamodb:GetItem`, `dynamodb:PutItem`, `dynamodb:UpdateItem`, `dynamodb:Query`; do not use `<table_arn>/index/*` or `<table_arn>/*` wildcards).\n     - Cryptographic usage (`kms:Decrypt`, `kms:GenerateDataKey`, `kms:DescribeKey`) on the `messaging` and `projection` KMS key ARNs only.\n     - CloudWatch Logs stream creation/writing scoped strictly to `logs.projector_log_group`.\n   - **Forbidden access (`Effect = \"Deny\"` required)**: Must not allow, and must explicitly deny via `Effect = \"Deny\"`, `sqs:SendMessage` on the main queue or `sqs:ReceiveMessage`/`sqs:DeleteMessage` on the DLQ; deleting DynamoDB items (`dynamodb:DeleteItem`) or tables (`dynamodb:DeleteTable`) on the projection table; any S3 access (`s3:GetObject`, `s3:PutObject`, `s3:DeleteObject`, `s3:ListBucket`) on `<audit_bucket_arn>` and `<audit_bucket_arn>/*`; `kms:Decrypt` or `kms:GenerateDataKey` on the `database` and `audit` KMS keys; and `logs:CreateLogStream` or `logs:PutLogEvents` on any non-projector log group (`api`, `relay`, `archiver`). Furthermore, must not grant `dynamodb:Query` on `<table_arn>/index/*`.\n4. **`relay_role_arn`**:\n   - **Trust principal**: `lambda.amazonaws.com`.\n   - **Allowed access (`Effect = \"Allow\"`)**:\n     - Publish-only access on the main SQS queue ARN (`sqs:SendMessage`, `sqs:GetQueueAttributes`, `sqs:GetQueueUrl`).\n     - Cryptographic usage (`kms:Decrypt`, `kms:GenerateDataKey`, `kms:DescribeKey`) on the `messaging` and `database` KMS key ARNs only.\n     - CloudWatch Logs stream creation/writing scoped strictly to `logs.relay_log_group`.\n   - **Forbidden access (`Effect = \"Deny\"` required)**: Must not allow, and must explicitly deny via `Effect = \"Deny\"`, `sqs:ReceiveMessage` or `sqs:DeleteMessage` on the main queue, `sqs:SendMessage`/`sqs:ReceiveMessage`/`sqs:DeleteMessage` on the DLQ, any DynamoDB actions (`dynamodb:GetItem`, `dynamodb:PutItem`, `dynamodb:UpdateItem`, `dynamodb:DeleteItem`, `dynamodb:Query`) on the projection table or `AccountIndex`, any S3 actions (`s3:GetObject`, `s3:PutObject`, `s3:DeleteObject`, `s3:ListBucket`) on `<audit_bucket_arn>` and `<audit_bucket_arn>/*`, `kms:Decrypt` or `kms:GenerateDataKey` on the `projection` and `audit` KMS keys, and `logs:CreateLogStream` or `logs:PutLogEvents` on any non-relay log group (`api`, `projector`, `archiver`).\n5. **`archiver_role_arn`**:\n   - **Trust principal**: `lambda.amazonaws.com`.\n   - **Allowed access (`Effect = \"Allow\"`)**:\n     - Append-only object access (`s3:PutObject`, `s3:GetObject`, `s3:AbortMultipartUpload`) scoped strictly to `<audit_bucket_arn>/ledger-audit/*`, and bucket-level metadata (`s3:ListBucket`, `s3:GetBucketLocation`) scoped strictly to `<audit_bucket_arn>` (keep object-level and bucket-level statements separated by resource type).\n     - Cryptographic usage (`kms:Decrypt`, `kms:GenerateDataKey`, `kms:DescribeKey`) on the `audit` and `database` KMS key ARNs only.\n     - CloudWatch Logs stream creation/writing scoped strictly to `logs.archiver_log_group`.\n   - **Forbidden access (`Effect = \"Deny\"` required)**: Must not grant `s3:PutObject` on the bucket ARN `<audit_bucket_arn>` or unscoped `<audit_bucket_arn>/*` root wildcard, and must not grant `s3:ListBucket` on object ARNs. Must explicitly deny via `Effect = \"Deny\"` `s3:DeleteObject` and `s3:DeleteObjectVersion` on `<audit_bucket_arn>` and `<audit_bucket_arn>/*`, any SQS actions (`sqs:SendMessage`, `sqs:ReceiveMessage`, `sqs:DeleteMessage`) on the main queue or DLQ, any DynamoDB actions (`dynamodb:GetItem`, `dynamodb:PutItem`, `dynamodb:UpdateItem`, `dynamodb:DeleteItem`, `dynamodb:Query`) on the projection table or `AccountIndex`, `kms:Decrypt` or `kms:GenerateDataKey` on the `messaging` and `projection` KMS keys, and `logs:CreateLogStream` or `logs:PutLogEvents` on any non-archiver log group (`api`, `projector`, `relay`).\n6. **`scheduler_role_arn`**:\n   - **Trust principal**: `scheduler.amazonaws.com`.\n   - **Allowed access (`Effect = \"Allow\"`)**:\n     - `lambda:InvokeFunction` scoped strictly to the Outbox Relay (`workers.outbox_relay.function_arn`) and Audit Archiver (`workers.audit_archiver.function_arn`) Lambda function ARNs.\n   - **Forbidden access (`Effect = \"Deny\"` required)**: Must not allow, and must explicitly deny via `Effect = \"Deny\"`, `lambda:InvokeFunction` on the Projector Lambda (`workers.projector.function_arn`), any SQS actions (`sqs:SendMessage`, `sqs:ReceiveMessage`, `sqs:DeleteMessage`) on the main queue or DLQ, any DynamoDB actions (`dynamodb:GetItem`, `dynamodb:PutItem`, `dynamodb:UpdateItem`, `dynamodb:DeleteItem`, `dynamodb:Query`) on the projection table or `AccountIndex`, any S3 actions (`s3:GetObject`, `s3:PutObject`, `s3:DeleteObject`, `s3:ListBucket`) on `<audit_bucket_arn>` and `<audit_bucket_arn>/*`, `kms:Decrypt` or `kms:GenerateDataKey` on all four KMS keys (`database`, `messaging`, `projection`, `audit`), and `logs:CreateLogStream` or `logs:PutLogEvents` on all four CloudWatch Log Groups (`api`, `projector`, `relay`, `archiver`).\n=== services/kms.md\n# Customer-Managed KMS Keys (`services/kms.md`)\n\nProvision four separate customer-managed KMS keys (`aws_kms_key`) and four KMS aliases (`aws_kms_alias`):\n\n- `kms.database_arn` with alias `alias/<resource_prefix>-database`: encrypts the RDS PostgreSQL instance\n- `kms.messaging_arn` with alias `alias/<resource_prefix>-messaging`: encrypts the main SQS queue and DLQ\n- `kms.projection_arn` with alias `alias/<resource_prefix>-projection`: encrypts the DynamoDB projection table\n- `kms.audit_arn` with alias `alias/<resource_prefix>-audit`: encrypts the S3 audit archive bucket\n\nEach of the four KMS keys must be configured with:\n- `enable_key_rotation = true`\n- `deletion_window_in_days = 10` (must be `10..30` days)\n- Tag `ClearLedgerDeployment = <resource_prefix>`\n\n=== services/lambda.md\n# Lambda Workers and Event Source Mapping (`services/lambda.md`)\n\nProvision three container-based AWS Lambda functions (`aws_lambda_function`, `package_type = \"Image\"`):\n\n1. **Projector (`workers.projector`)**:\n   - `image_uri = projector_image` from `config.json`\n   - `role = iam.projector_role_arn`\n   - Environment variables: `AWS_ENDPOINT_URL`, `PROJECTION_TABLE`, `VALKEY_URL`, `CLOUDWATCH_LOG_GROUP`\n   - Wired to the main SQS queue via an `aws_lambda_event_source_mapping` with:\n     - `enabled = true`\n     - `batch_size = 5`\n     - `maximum_batching_window_in_seconds = 0`\n     - `function_response_types = [\"ReportBatchItemFailures\"]`\n2. **Outbox Relay (`workers.outbox_relay`)**:\n   - `image_uri = relay_image` from `config.json`\n   - `role = iam.relay_role_arn`\n   - Environment variables: `AWS_ENDPOINT_URL`, `DATABASE_URL`, `SQS_QUEUE_URL`, `OUTBOX_BATCH_SIZE = \"50\"`, `CLOUDWATCH_LOG_GROUP`\n3. **Audit Archiver (`workers.audit_archiver`)**:\n   - `image_uri = archiver_image` from `config.json`\n   - `role = iam.archiver_role_arn`\n   - Environment variables: `AWS_ENDPOINT_URL`, `DATABASE_URL`, `AUDIT_BUCKET`, `AUDIT_PREFIX = \"ledger-audit/\"`, `CLOUDWATCH_LOG_GROUP`\n=== services/rds.md\n# RDS PostgreSQL (`services/rds.md`)\n\n- Provision a DB subnet group (`aws_db_subnet_group`) spanning strictly the private subnets (`network.private_subnet_ids`).\n- Provision a single PostgreSQL 16 RDS instance (`aws_db_instance`) with:\n  - `engine = \"postgres\"`\n  - `engine_version = \"16.3\"` (or `16.*`)\n  - `instance_class = \"db.t4g.micro\"`\n  - `db_name`, `username`, and `password` from `/workspace/config/config.json`\n  - `storage_encrypted = true`\n  - `kms_key_id` set to the customer-managed `database` KMS key ARN (`kms.database_arn`)\n  - `publicly_accessible = false`\n  - `skip_final_snapshot = true`\n  - `vpc_security_group_ids` containing the `rds` security group\n- Re-running `deploy.sh` must never replace the RDS instance or lose committed data.\n\n## Required Database Schema, Relational Invariants, and Triggers\n\nThe pre-built Rust binaries (`clearledger-api`, `clearledger-outbox-relay`, and `clearledger-audit-archiver`) do **not** run database migrations on startup. `clearledger-api` relies on PostgreSQL `CHECK`/`UNIQUE`/`FOREIGN KEY` constraints and PL/pgSQL triggers to enforce domain, schema, and state-transition invariants at the database layer (mapping SQLSTATE `23514`, `23503`, `23502`, `23505`, and `P0001` exceptions to HTTP `400 Bad Request`), and `GET /health/ready` verifies that all four tables, all three indexes, and user-defined triggers on all four tables exist in the `clearledger` schema before reporting `checks.postgres = \"UP\"`.\n\n`deploy.sh` must idempotently initialize the `clearledger` schema, all four tables, their constraints, their triggers, and all three indexes on the RDS PostgreSQL instance.\n\n### 1. Table `clearledger.settlements`\n\n- **Columns**:\n  - `settlement_id UUID NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY`\n  - `account_id TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `reference TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `debit_party TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `credit_party TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `current_status TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `current_stage TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `last_entry_id UUID NULL`\n  - `last_memo TEXT NULL`\n  - `version INTEGER NOT NULL`\n  - `entry_count INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0`\n  - `created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()`\n  - `updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()`\n- **Required invariants (enforced via `CHECK` constraints and `BEFORE UPDATE` trigger)**:\n  - Trimmed string length bounds and allowed status values must match `CreateSettlementRequest` and `AppendEntryRequest` in `openapi.yaml` (including `1..256` trimmed characters when `last_memo` is non-null), with trimmed `debit_party <> credit_party`, `version >= 1`, and monotonic timestamps (`updated_at >= created_at` on insert and `NEW.updated_at >= OLD.updated_at` on update).\n  - At initiation (`version = 1`), `entry_count = 0`, `current_status = 'INITIATED'`, `last_entry_id IS NULL`, and `updated_at = created_at`. For `version > 1`, `entry_count = version - 1`, `current_status <> 'INITIATED'`, and `last_entry_id IS NOT NULL`.\n  - On `UPDATE`: header columns (`settlement_id`, `account_id`, `reference`, `debit_party`, `credit_party`, `created_at`) are immutable; each update must step `version` and `entry_count` by `+1` with a new `last_entry_id` (`NEW.last_entry_id IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.last_entry_id`); and `current_status` must follow the clearing lifecycle progression:\n    - The non-`DISPUTED` clearing status rank order is `INITIATED` (`0`) < `VALIDATED` (`1`) < `RESERVED` (`2`) < `CLEARED` (`3`) < `SETTLED` (`4`) < `RECONCILED` (`5`).\n    - `RECONCILED` is strictly terminal: once `OLD.current_status = 'RECONCILED'`, any `UPDATE` on the settlement row must be rejected (even if `NEW.current_status = 'RECONCILED'`).\n    - From any non-`RECONCILED`, non-`DISPUTED` status (`INITIATED`, `VALIDATED`, `RESERVED`, `CLEARED`, `SETTLED`), `NEW.current_status` may transition to `'DISPUTED'` or to any status whose rank is monotonic non-decreasing (`rank(NEW.current_status) >= rank(OLD.current_status)` — permitting same-status updates such as `CLEARED` -> `CLEARED` and single- or multi-stage forward advances such as `INITIATED` -> `VALIDATED` or `INITIATED` -> `CLEARED`, while rejecting any backward rank regression where `rank(NEW.current_status) < rank(OLD.current_status)` such as `CLEARED` -> `RESERVED`).\n    - From `OLD.current_status = 'DISPUTED'`, `NEW.current_status` may only remain `'DISPUTED'` or resolve to terminal `'RECONCILED'`.\n\n### 2. Table `clearledger.events`\n\n- **Columns**:\n  - `seq BIGSERIAL NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY`\n  - `event_id UUID NOT NULL UNIQUE`\n  - `settlement_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES clearledger.settlements(settlement_id) ON DELETE CASCADE`\n  - `aggregate_version INTEGER NOT NULL`\n  - `event_type TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `correlation_id TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `idempotency_key TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `occurred_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL`\n  - `payload JSONB NOT NULL`\n  - `created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()`\n- **Required invariants (enforced via `UNIQUE`/`CHECK` constraints and `BEFORE INSERT`/`UPDATE`/`DELETE` triggers)**:\n  - Composite `UNIQUE (settlement_id, aggregate_version)` and `UNIQUE (settlement_id, idempotency_key)`, with trimmed length bounds on `correlation_id` and `idempotency_key` matching `openapi.yaml`.\n  - `payload` must conform strictly to `ClearLedgerDomainEventEnvelope` in `schemas/events.schema.json` (including `additionalProperties: false` on both the envelope and `payload.data`, required non-null `accountId`, `reference`, `debitParty`, `creditParty`, `status`, and `clearingStage` in `payload.data` with trimmed string length bounds matching `openapi.yaml` and `1..256` trimmed characters when `data.memo` is non-null, column-to-envelope equality across `event_id`, `settlement_id`, `aggregate_version`, `event_type`, `correlation_id`, `idempotency_key`, and `occurred_at` compared as `TIMESTAMPTZ`, and version/kind/status/entryId coupling for `SettlementInitiated` at `v = 1` vs `LedgerEntryRecorded` at `v >= 2`).\n  - On `INSERT`: per-settlement `aggregate_version` must be contiguous starting at `1`; `data.entryId` must be unique per settlement across all `LedgerEntryRecorded` events; the event must match the parent row in `clearledger.settlements` (including header fields, `current_status`, `current_stage`, `last_entry_id`, `last_memo`, `version`, and `occurred_at = updated_at`, plus `occurred_at = created_at` at `v = 1`); and for `aggregate_version >= 2`, `NEW.occurred_at` must be `>=` the immediately preceding event's `occurred_at` (`aggregate_version = NEW.aggregate_version - 1`) and the status transition from that preceding event's `data.status` to `NEW.payload->'data'->>'status'` must satisfy the settlement status transition rules above (monotonic non-decreasing rank, reachable `DISPUTED` from non-`RECONCILED`, `DISPUTED` only to `DISPUTED` or `RECONCILED`, and no transitions out of or repeating `RECONCILED`).\n  - `clearledger.events` is strictly append-only (`UPDATE` and `DELETE` must be rejected).\n\n### 3. Table `clearledger.outbox`\n\n- **Columns**:\n  - `seq BIGSERIAL NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY`\n  - `event_id UUID NOT NULL UNIQUE REFERENCES clearledger.events(event_id) ON DELETE CASCADE`\n  - `settlement_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES clearledger.settlements(settlement_id) ON DELETE CASCADE`\n  - `aggregate_version INTEGER NOT NULL`\n  - `correlation_id TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `payload JSONB NOT NULL`\n  - `created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()`\n  - `published_at TIMESTAMPTZ NULL`\n  - `archived_at TIMESTAMPTZ NULL`\n  - `attempts INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0`\n  - `last_error TEXT NULL`\n- **Required invariants (enforced via `UNIQUE`/`FOREIGN KEY`/`CHECK` constraints and triggers)**:\n  - Composite `UNIQUE (settlement_id, aggregate_version)` and `FOREIGN KEY (settlement_id, aggregate_version) REFERENCES clearledger.events(settlement_id, aggregate_version) ON DELETE CASCADE`.\n  - `payload` must conform to `ClearLedgerDomainEventEnvelope` (`schemas/events.schema.json`) and on `INSERT` must exactly mirror the corresponding row in `clearledger.events` in contiguous per-settlement `aggregate_version` order starting at `1`.\n  - Delivery/archival lifecycle: `attempts >= 0`; when `published_at IS NOT NULL`, `attempts >= 1`, `last_error IS NULL`, and `published_at >= created_at`; `archived_at` requires `published_at IS NOT NULL` and `archived_at >= published_at`.\n  - `DELETE` is forbidden; on `UPDATE`, envelope columns are immutable, `attempts` cannot decrease, publishing an unpublished row requires incrementing `attempts` with `archived_at IS NULL`, while `published_at` remains `NOT NULL` (such as during archival or resetting `archived_at = NULL` for S3 re-archival), `published_at`, `attempts`, and `last_error` cannot be mutated (resetting `published_at = NULL, archived_at = NULL` for operational replay is permitted), and once `OLD.archived_at IS NOT NULL` and `NEW.archived_at IS NOT NULL`, `NEW.archived_at` cannot be mutated to a different timestamp without first resetting `archived_at = NULL`.\n\n### 4. Table `clearledger.idempotency_keys`\n\n- **Columns**:\n  - `scope TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `idempotency_key TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `request_hash TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `status_code INTEGER NOT NULL`\n  - `response_body JSONB NOT NULL`\n  - `created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()`\n  - Composite `PRIMARY KEY (scope, idempotency_key)`\n- **Required invariants (enforced via `CHECK` constraints and triggers)**:\n  - `scope` (`create:<settlement_uuid>` or `entry:<settlement_uuid>`), `idempotency_key` (`8..128` trimmed chars), lowercase hex SHA-256 `request_hash`, `status_code` (`201` for `create:*` at `version = 1`; `202` for `entry:*` at `version >= 2`), and closed-schema `response_body` (`WriteAcceptedResponse` in `openapi.yaml` with `accepted = true` and `idempotentReplay = false`).\n  - On `INSERT`, the referenced event (`eventId`, `settlementId`, `version`, `idempotency_key`) must exist in both `clearledger.events` and `clearledger.outbox`. `UPDATE` and `DELETE` must be rejected.\n\n### 5. Required Indexes in `clearledger` Schema\n\n- `idx_clearledger_outbox_unpublished`: on `clearledger.outbox (seq)` with partial predicate `WHERE published_at IS NULL`\n- `idx_clearledger_outbox_unarchived`: on `clearledger.outbox (seq)` with partial predicate `WHERE published_at IS NOT NULL AND archived_at IS NULL`\n- `idx_clearledger_events_settlement_version`: on `clearledger.events (settlement_id, aggregate_version)`\n=== services/s3.md\n# S3 Audit Archive (`services/s3.md`)\n\n- Provision a dedicated S3 bucket (`aws_s3_bucket`) for NDJSON audit batches 'INITIATED'  THEN 0\n    WHEN 'VALIDATED'  THEN 1\n    WHEN 'RESERVED'   THEN 2\n    WHEN 'CLEARED'    THEN 3\n    WHEN 'SETTLED'    THEN 4\n    WHEN 'RECONCILED' THEN 5\n    ELSE NULL\n  END\n$$;\n\n-- Clearing lifecycle transition rule shared by settlements and events.\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clearledger.status_transition_ok(old_status text, new_status text)\nRETURNS boolean LANGUAGE sql IMMUTABLE PARALLEL SAFE AS $$\n  SELECT CASE\n    WHEN old_status IS NULL OR new_status IS NULL THEN false\n    WHEN old_status = 'RECONCILED' THEN false\n    WHEN old_status = 'DISPUTED' THEN new_status IN ('DISPUTED', 'RECONCILED')\n    WHEN clearledger.status_rank(old_status) IS NULL THEN false\n    WHEN new_status = 'DISPUTED' THEN true\n    WHEN clearledger.status_rank(new_status) IS NULL THEN false\n    ELSE clearledger.status_rank(new_status) >= clearledger.status_rank(old_status)\n  END\n$$;\n\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clearledger.is_rfc3339(v text)\nRETURNS boolean LANGUAGE plpgsql IMMUTABLE PARALLEL SAFE AS $$\nDECLARE\n  ts timestamptz;\nBEGIN\n  IF v IS NULL OR v !~ '^[0-9]{4}-[0-9]{2}-[0-9]{2}[Tt][0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}(\\.[0-9]+)?([Zz]|[+-][0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2})$' THEN\n    RETURN false;\n  END IF;\n  ts := v::timestamptz;\n  RETURN ts IS NOT NULL;\nEXCEPTION WHEN others THEN\n  RETURN false;\nEND\n$$;\n\n-- Strict validation of ClearLedgerDomainEventEnvelope (schemas/events.schema.json).\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clearledger.is_valid_envelope(p entry_id jsonb;\n  memo     jsonb;\nBEGIN\n  IF p IS NULL OR jsonb_typeof(p) <> 'object' THEN\n    RETURN false;\n  END IF;\n\n  -- envelope: closed schema, all keys required\n  FOR k IN SELECT
